# ViewTouch Modern — development notes

A running, time-stamped record of changes made to this repository by each contributor
(people, Bob (Claude), Neil (Gemini)). It complements `CHANGELOG.md`, which is generated automatically from
commits by `tools/changelog.py` — don't hand-edit that one.

**How to add an entry**
- Add new entries at the **top** of the log (newest first).
- Heading: `### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM ET — <who> — <short title>` (Eastern time, the store's clock).
- Say **what** changed, **why**, the **files**, and the **commit** (short hash) once pushed.
- Mention anything the next person must know (breaking changes, steps to run on the Pi).
- Pull before editing and commit this file together with the change it describes.
- Quickest: stage your change, then run
  `python3 modern/tools/log_dev_note.py --agent <who> --title "..." --what "..." --why "..." [--next "..."]`.
- **Enforced:** `.githooks/pre-commit` blocks any commit that changes files without a new, complete entry at
  the top of this Log. Turn it on once per clone with `bash modern/tools/setup_dev_hooks.sh`.
  Rules for everyone (people, Bob (Claude), Neil (Gemini)) are in `AGENTS.md` (also `CLAUDE.md`, `GEMINI.md`).

---

## 1. Shared Engineering & Logging Standard (`Neil` & `Bob`)

* **Canonical File**: `modern/vt-modern-development-notes.md` (never hand-edit `CHANGELOG.md`, which is generated by `tools/changelog.py`).
* **Agent Names**: **Neil (Gemini)** and **Bob (Claude)**. Keep literal file/product names (`CLAUDE.md`, `GEMINI.md`, `claude_label_templates.json`) unchanged.
* **Entry Format**: Prepend every change newest-first at the top of `## Log` using `### YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM ET — <Agent> — <short title>` with bullets **What**, **Why**, **Files** (required; the hook checks them), plus **Commit** and **Next person must know** when there is something to say.
* **Git Hook & Discipline**: Always `git pull` before editing. `.githooks/pre-commit` and `modern/tools/log_dev_note.py` block commits that touch repo files unless `modern/vt-modern-development-notes.md` is staged with a valid entry. Do not auto-generate empty skeleton entries or rewrite commit hashes post-commit.

## 2. Technical Handoff & Invariants ("Next Person Must Know")

* **Canonical Repo Path**: Always build and run from `/home/pi/viewtouch-main/modern` (never the legacy `~/Desktop/viewtouch-main` directory). Pi commands for the owner start with `cd ~/viewtouch-main` and end with `; beep3`.
* **viewtouch.db belongs to ViewTouch**: Live DB `~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/viewtouch.db`. ViewTouch keeps the store in memory and writes it back, so no other program may write to the DB while it runs (changes are lost or overwritten). The label utility opens it read-only (`mode=ro`). Any bulk import (menu, gift cards) must stop ViewTouch, back up the DB, then restart.
* **Branches**: `main` (what the Pi runs), `claude-label-utility` (Bob's working branch; `modern/tools/update_label_utility.sh` on the Pi merges it into `main` and pushes), and upload-only branches written by Pi scripts: `pi-shots` (label utility screenshots), `pi-vt-shots` (ViewTouch screenshots, `capture_vt_screenshots.sh`), `pi-logs` (`push_label_log.sh`). Bob's cloud workspace can't build or run Qt, so anything visual is captured on the Pi and fetched from these branches.
* **QML module loading**: The static `ViewTouch` QML module is built without the QTP0001 policy, so it lives under `qrc:/ViewTouch`, not `qrc:/qt/qml/ViewTouch`; `loadFromModule("ViewTouch", ...)` can't find it. Load screens by file as `main.cpp` (`vtmLoadModuleQml`) and the screenshot test (`loadViewTouchQml` in `tests/test_pos_flow.cpp`) do. `tests/test_pages_ui.cpp` and `tests/test_editor_ui.cpp` still use `loadFromModule` and fail on the Pi.
* **Compilation & SQLite WAL Safety**: The Pi 5 has 4 cores and 8 GB: compile with `-j4` (vt-server does; `VT_JOBS=2 vt-server` to use fewer) with the 2 GB swap file and ccache set up on 2026-11-03. Older advice was `-j1`/`-j2` (OOM with 200 MB swap). Never kill `vtmodern` while SQLite is writing in WAL mode (`viewtouch.db-wal`) without cleaning up `.lock` and `.running` files in `~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/`.
* **Zero-Header-Breakage Persistence**: Custom item attributes (`__retail__`, `__cost__`, `__parent__`, and upcoming `__pour_oz__` / `__abv__`) are stored inside `m.recipe` metadata rather than modifying core C++ struct headers, preventing full-tree recompiles and preserving backward/forward database compatibility.
* **Known Fragile Spots**:
  * `modern/android/AndroidManifest.xml`: `<activity-alias android:name="org.viewtouch.pos.Home">` must retain both `<meta-data android:name="android.app.lib_name".../>` lines or Lenovo tablets crash with `UnsatisfiedLinkError` on cold boot.
  * `modern/tools/install_label_utility.sh`: Never delete `/home/pi/Desktop/*label*.desktop` by wildcard, as it deletes the operator's `Update Label Utility` desktop icon.
  * Retail screen "Gift Cards" button: the page is `gift-card` (singular). Fixed 2026-11-02 in both `modern/seed/pages/retail-entry.json` and `modern/ui/qml/WidgetRetailEntry.qml`, which had `gift-cards`.
  * `vt-directv`: Page jump actions require both `"page"` and `"target"` keys.

## 3. Live Environment & Network Topology

* **Main POS Server**: Raspberry Pi 5 (`192.168.1.219`, MAC `d8:3a:dd:e5:47:a5`, POS TCP port `7719`, migrating to `192.168.6.x` on UniFi Dream Machine) running Debian 13 Trixie (Qt 6.8.2). Live DB: `~/.local/share/ViewTouch/ViewTouch/viewtouch.db`.
* **Lenovo Android Tablet Fleet**: Bar 1 (`192.168.1.220`), Bar 2 (`192.168.1.215`), and Bar 3 (`192.168.1.210`) managed via wireless ADB port `5555` (`vt-adb-ensure`, `vt-status`, `vt-server`, `vt-tablets`). Their addresses are fixed in the UniFi backend.
* **Printers**: Epson TM-T88 receipt/kitchen thermal printer at `192.168.1.77:9100` (ESC/POS); ZPL label printers "Shelf Labels" at `192.168.1.24:9100` (2x1 vinyl price labels; CUPS queue `BHDAN_PRICE_LABEL`) and "Stick Labels" at `192.168.1.25:9100` (cigar stick labels, different stock).
* **This repository is public** (`paigeent1/viewtouch`): never put passwords, PINs, API keys or customer data in the repo or these notes.

## 4. Codified Summary of Completed `vt-modern` Customizations

* **Retail POS, Inventory Workstation & Barcode Engine**:
  * Dedicated Retail POS Entry Mode (`ui/qml/WidgetRetailEntry.qml`, `seed/pages/retail-entry.json`) with 11x6 tactile matrix, PLU keypad, BOX <-> STICK toggle, 21+ age check modal, 5-level merchandise tree browser, 1-tap Retail <-> Hospitality switching, and per-terminal default mode (`startPage` / `vt-set-mode`).
  * Full-Screen Retail Item Workstation (`ui/qml/WidgetRetailItemEditor.qml`, `WidgetMenuBuilder.qml`) with 3-way mode chooser (Hospitality, Retail, Events), category mode toggles (`F&B`, `Retail`, `Event`), Alpha vs. UPC sort, `isRetail` flag (`{"__retail__", 1.0}`), 5-level hierarchy, parent/child unit cost cascade (`__cost__`, `__parent__`), and live Profit/Margin/Markup math.
  * Primary UPC (`m.barcodes[0]`) & Alt UPC (`m.barcodes[1..N]`) mapping across `app/pos_admin_*.cpp`, `pos_menu*.cpp`, and `WidgetMenuBuilder.qml`; GS1 Modulo-10 check-digit verification (`PosService::barcodeProblem()` in `app/pos_service.cpp`) with 11-to-12-digit auto-completion (`layoutcontroller.cpp`); and Unrecognized Barcode overlay (`u"unrecognized"_s` in `app/pos_loyalty.cpp` -> `ScanAddOverlay.qml`).
* **ViewTouch Label Utility v1.0 & Documentation**:
  * Unified `modern/tools/vt_label_utility.py` (`__version__ = "1.0"`, tag `label-utility-v1.0`), 100% read-only SQLite access, 203 DPI 1-bit full-label bitmap rendering (`^GFA`), integer-dot UPC-A, EAN-13, UPC-E, Code 39 and Code 128 barcodes, in-memory Primary/Alt/Carton UPC selection, `VTRoundedButton` dark UI, `Delete Template` & `Restore Standard Labels` buttons (added after the `label-utility-v1.0` tag, which is at `9f1c09f`), and `install_label_utility.sh` / `update_label_utility.sh`.
  * 24-page operator manual (`modern/docs/label-utility/ViewTouch_Label_Utility_User_Manual.pdf`, `build_manual.py` with `stack_toolbar`) and automated screenshot harvester (`capture_manual_screenshots.sh` -> `myfork/pi-shots`).
* **Printed Receipt & Touchscreen Choice Formatting**:
  * Always print free modifier choices (`print/tickets.cpp`), ESC/POS Font B (`9x17` dots, `\x1b\x4d\x01`, `4/3` width scaling) for 2-space indented sub-lines and `Opened <time> - closed <time>` timestamps (`print/document.cpp`), and compact `w.unit * 0.65` modifier text in `ui/qml/WidgetOrderList.qml`.
* **Customer Tracking, Gift Cards & Reporting**:
  * Split First/Last Name customer fields, cumulative spend/visit tracking, 1-tap closed-check customer assignment, and migration of 5,538 legacy customer profiles from `ilsa.mdb`.
  * 4-argument `PosService::sellGiftCard(number, amountCents, purchasedBy, issuedTo)` (`app/pos_customers.cpp`, `pos_service.hh`; the names are applied only to an existing card, and the Gift Card screen doesn't pass them, so PIN/For/By come from the PatronPal import; there is no bulk gift card import tool), `giftCardCashBelowCents` threshold, 8-column landscape gift card reports (Activated, Active Balances, Inactive, Transactions) (`core/report.cpp`, `ui/cpp/reportexport.cpp`, `ui/qml/WidgetReportView.qml`), 15-page manual with Pi screenshots (`modern/docs/gift-cards/ViewTouch_Gift_Cards_User_Manual.pdf`, `build_manual.py`), and offscreen screenshot runner (`modern/tools/capture_vt_screenshots.sh` -> `myfork/pi-vt-shots`).
* **Hospitality, Recipes, Check Splitting & DirecTV**:
  * Bill of Materials (`ingredients.json`) recipe costing/depletion, integer-cents check splitting with remainder penny allocation, and customized DirecTV seed pages (`seed/pages/directv-tune-*.json`).
* **Top Status Ribbon, UI Fit Fixes & Android Tablet Kiosk Fleet**:
  * Top Status Ribbon (`topRibbonEnabled`, time, battery, Wi-Fi, customer count, and live ZIP weather via `storeZip` / `fetchWeather` across `core/settings.hh`, `app/pos_session.hh`, `app/pos_service.hh`, `net/remote_session.hh`, `app/pos_admin_fields.cpp`, and `ui/qml/Main.qml`).
  * 64-bit (`arm64-v8a`) APK pipeline (`.github/workflows/modern-android.yml`, `build_apk.sh`, `cmake/BuildInfo.cmake`), Kiosk home alias (`AndroidManifest.xml`, `Kiosk.kt`), `ensureWirelessAdb`, PIN-protected reboot (`rebootWithPin`), yellow Terminal Name badge, JNI camera scanner (`BarcodeScanner.kt` -> `scanCamera`), and upstream fixes (`2e37ab3a` wake-from-sleep connection, `96b336f8` first-staff-login freeze fix).

Not started unless a Log entry says otherwise; files named here (`CustomerReceiptView.qml`, `stage_batch.py`, `corp_sync.py`, `store_config.json`) don't exist yet.

* **Front-End Ticket Counter, Mode Buttons & Importers**: Live item/quantity counter (`Items: X (Qty: Y)`) in `WidgetOrderList.qml`, Cashier/Manager mode buttons, variable-weight scale barcode parsing, separate `UPC`/`ALTUPC` CSV columns (`app/menu_import.cpp`), and ILSA batch staging (`stage_batch.py`).
* **Secondary HDMI Customer-Facing Display (CFD)**: Split-screen mode (`--customer-display window`) on Pi 5 Micro-HDMI 1 with 50% live receipt (`CustomerReceiptView.qml`) and 50% dynamic promo/pairing panel (`goesWith`).
  *Already working (not this item):* the basic customer display (`ui/qml/CustomerDisplay.qml`, `CustomerDisplayWindow.qml`: order, total, tips, thank-you, price checks), turned on in Manager -> Terminals -> Customer display (runs `vt-guest-display on`: HDMI-2 beside HDMI-1, not mirrored). Don't launch vtmodern with `--customer-display` while the monitors are mirrored.
* **Tablet customer displays with video (future idea)**: The Bar 1/2/3 tablets have no customer display today. If they get one later: add `-m qtmultimedia` to the Android workflow's `aqt install-qt` so `VideoPanel.qml` builds into the APK, and serve the Store Settings > Customer display video (an MP4 on the Pi) to the tablets over the network (a file on the Pi isn't reachable from a tablet). Until then tablets fall back to the picture/GIF.
* **Guest-screen raffles & contact entry (owner idea, 2026-11-03)**: With a second Wimaxit as a touch guest screen: guests type phone and email (needs an on-screen keyboard on the guest page, not just the phone keypad), and enter raffles; each entry prints legibly on a raffle-ticket printer (name, phone/email, drawing, number), and the entries are kept for the drawing.
* **Date of birth on receipts (owner decision 2026-11-06; not started)**: Print the buyer's date of birth on the receipt when an ID scan checked the age, as ILSA does. Today ViewTouch keeps the age passed, how, cashier, time, expired flag and the last 4 of the ID number on the check: it must also keep the DOB from the scan, print it on the receipt and on reprints (Receipt Lookup); legacy ILSA receipts carry theirs (imported with the history). Never in the repo, never in reports or exports; tests use made-up dates.
* **Customer accounts: key fob number, cell phone, ID link (owner 2026-11-05; not started)**: One customer record, found three ways: (1) a unique *customer number* printed as a barcode on a key fob or card (scan it and the customer is on the sale); (2) the *cell phone number*, typed by the customer on the guest screen or by the cashier; (3) *the customer's license*: an ID scan tied to the customer. Open questions for the owner: pre-numbered fobs or ViewTouch-issued numbers (gift cards start with 6, subscription cards with 9: fobs their own leading digit, or the subscription card doubles as the fob?); what sign-up requires (phone only, or name too); several fobs per customer (lost fob, spouse).
* **Price book name cleanup: "6+" means 6⅛ (owner 2026-11-05; not started; TOP of the list)**: *Changed 2026-11-06 (owner approved): names are fixed in ViewTouch, not ILSA (longer names; ⅛ ⅜ ⅝ ⅞ stored as is), on a "Price book cleanup" screen: lists names with 6+, 6++, a stray +, written-out fractions; suggested fix beside each (6+ X 52 -> 6⅛ X 52); approve all / edit one / skip; then fills Length / Ring gauge / Ring gauge 2 from the cleaned names with a preview. New items arriving from ILSA before cutover get the same suggestions.* The original idea below (an import rule) is kept only for those new items. In ILSA the owner typed "+" for an eighth (e.g. "6+" = 6 1/8; ILSA's Windows character set has no ⅛). Build it as a rule in vt-ilsa-items (applied on every import, so the cutover re-import keeps it), not a one-time edit: a number followed by "+" (regex (\d)\+(?!\d)) becomes an eighth, with a list of exceptions the owner marks (e.g. "2+1" deals, "+ LIGHTER", "6+ PK" if any). First a read-only review list (~/ilsa-items/plus-review.csv: UPC, name now, would become; Bob gave the owner the command). Open question: "6⅛" (compact; screens and labels show ⅛, receipts print 6 1/8; printable() maps ⅛ → 1/8 since 2026-11-05) or "6 1/8" (plain, longer on 2x1 labels). Same pass: ¢ ½ ¼ ¾ already come through since the Windows-1252 fix. *Also in the same pass (owner: "automation is key")*: fill the new cigar size fields (length, ringGauge, ringGauge2; added 2026-11-05) from names that spell the size, e.g. "6+ X 52", "6 1/8 X 52", "5X50", "6+X52/60" (length: whole + optional fraction, ½ ¼ ¾ or + = 1/8; then X; then the gauge, optional /second gauge for diademas); only fills empty fields, never overwrites a size typed in ViewTouch; same review list first (UPC, name, length, gauge, gauge 2) so odd matches (e.g. "2X50 PK" counts, "5X" pack sizes) can be marked as exceptions.
* **Test suite health (found 2026-11-03)**: GitHub "Modern checks" hasn't passed in 100+ runs. Fixed tonight: screens now load in tests (qml_load.hh) and a crash cut the run short. Still failing on main: the Menu Builder screen tests can't find item buttons (builderItem-<id>) in its grid (about 15 tests), and the gift-card page logs a Qt Quick Layouts "recursive rearrange" (WidgetGiftCard.qml; wrapped Labels already given Layout.preferredWidth). Plus a few unrelated (versions, forecast, self-order slip).
* **Retail receiving, inventory counts & movement (owner decisions 2026-11-04; not started)**: Goal: inventory counts right and movement data to plan purchases. No manager PIN anywhere in this (cashier or manager may be on duty; who did what is logged). No purchase orders (back orders make them confusing): receive what is on the invoice and in the box; no short / back-order list. Build in this order:
  1. *Receiving by scan*: pick the vendor, scan items (repeat scan +1, or type a count; case/carton UPCs add their pack size); quantities always go to on-hand. An item not on file: link the code to an existing item (saved as an alt UPC), quick-add (UPC filled in; name, department, cost, price, pack size), or set aside and finish at the end. Free goods (e.g. B7G1) are received as quantity. *Swap / adjustment* receiving for damaged cigars traded with reps (usually not the same product): + and - lines, no cost, a reason; counts net out. After saving, offer shelf labels for new items.
  2. *Movement screen* by item, brand, vendor: on hand, sold last 4/13/52 weeks, per week, weeks of supply, last received, suggested order (target weeks of supply minus on hand; a guide, not a PO), dead stock (no sales in 60/90 days), same weeks last year once there's history (or ILSA's, if imported). Physical counts (Stock Count) record differences as shrink.
  3. *System cost from the owner's worksheet*: he keeps what he wants system cost to be in a worksheet; import it (CSV/xlsx, preview, apply). Receiving NEVER changes system cost or the price book. OTP is already in his costs: no OTP handling at all.
  4. *Cost Differences review* (on screen, actionable): each line keeps invoice unit cost when the ticket shows it; difference vs system cost per unit and total; by vendor / brand (manufacturer > brand > product group) / item, with a summary that shows a brand-wide increase (e.g. 14 of 14 items up $0.18); lines can be ticked Reviewed; each vendor's last price per item kept for reference (same product from two vendors: system cost stays the one he chose). From a brand or vendor: *Adjust prices* (new cost: invoice / +$ / +% / unchanged; new retail: keep markup, +$, +%, hit target margin, or typed; rounding to his price endings; preview, untick, apply; offer labels).
  New fields: retail item system cost and *target margin %* (per item, default by department/brand; reports flag items under target). Vendor list exists (Manager > Inventory > Vendors: name, phone, account #, note): add rep name/cell, email, ordering method, active. Bring ILSA's vendor list and any target margins over with the ILSA data (owner is getting the ILSA database from Dropbox). Open questions for the owner: markup % vs same $ pass-through on cost increases; price endings by department; hide tiny cost differences or show all.
* **ILSA history import & beta tie-out (owner decisions 2026-11-04; importer and reports built 2026-11-07, branch bob/ilsa-history)**: *Built 2026-11-07:* `scripts/bin/vt-ilsa-history EXPORT [--dry-run] [--from/--to YYYY-MM-DD] [--replace]` imports the receipts, lines, tenders and ILSA's Z numbers into their own file, `ilsa-history.db` next to viewtouch.db (not viewtouch.db: it stays small, its backups and the standby copy don't carry 850k old lines; the history file can be rebuilt from the export any time). One transaction per business day (an ILSA Z, dated by its first receipt); re-runs skip days already in (an export's newest day is kept as partial and replaced by a newer export); real runs stop ViewTouch, back up the file, import, start ViewTouch again. Self-check per Z against Z_Taxes, Z_Tender (AMT_TEND; cash less all change), RegReconcile paid outs and the Z_Totals amounts (their meaning learned from the days), list in ~/ilsa-history/variances-<when>.csv and on the ILSA Differences report. Tenders are imported exactly as ILSA recorded them, never fixed (owner 2026-11-07: the 9/24 $90.66 card-batch gap is a sale rung as cash that was paid on the standalone card machine). Reports > ILSA History (new page): Daily Z (an ILSA day, ViewTouch's own checks that day too), Departments (when sold by default, owner 2026-11-07; "as ILSA: item's current department" to match the printouts), Item Movement (item / UPC / brand / department), GP by month / year, ILSA vs ViewTouch (ILSA's Z, the imported receipts, ViewTouch, with variances), ILSA Differences; Print, CSV, PDF. GP shown twice: as ILSA (lines without a cost at cost = retail, Adjusted Cost = Cost + Buydowns) and without no-cost lines (the true margin). Look Up Checks > ILSA history: receipts by day / # / UPC / total / name, read-only, marked ILSA; reprint as ILSA printed it (with the DOB, Store Settings' manager rule applies) or without. *First real import (owner, 2026-11-07, main 262ee15): ILSA 618 days, kp 1,822, copy bhdan 4,445; 6,885 days in all. `vt-ilsa-history --recheck` (branch bob/ilsa-recheck) redoes the self-check from the history file: receipts checked against lines + tax less their receipt discount; tenders only on Z's where ILSA saved tenders; the 2023-02-02 Z 5828 cash is labelled as the known mistyped amount; it prints what explains each kind of difference and the days whose dates look wrong (the history reported a 2004 first day: to be explained from its output).* *Stores (2026-11-07):* each ILSA copy belongs to a store (legacy_sources.store: BHDAN, BHNM next with `--prefix BHNM`); days are kept per store (one store's copy never replaces or skips another's); an export whose Sys_Pram names another store is refused unless --force; ILSA History, its Daily Z, Look Up Checks > ILSA history and the self-check (--recheck --store) choose a store or All, starting with Store Settings' 'ILSA history store' (default BHDAN). *Cashiers (2026-11-07, branch bob/ilsa-cashiers):* the importer keeps each copy's cashier numbers and names (no PINs or pay); Reports > ILSA History > Cashiers ties an ILSA cashier to a ViewTouch employee (or leaves them a former employee; the same number and name in the other copies follow); ILSA History > By Employee (sales by employee and item, with ViewTouch's own sales beside them) and the Reports screen's By Employee '+ ILSA history' switch use those ties. *Still open:* ILSA's Department Sales "Discount" column (receipt-level percent / subtotal discounts) isn't found yet: the importer keeps any DISC...AMT column of RegSale / PDetail and prints which it found; ViewTouch days have no cost per line or department at sale yet (comes with the CheckLine cost change). Before: The owner's ILSA database (Access .mdb) goes back to 2009; he downloads it from Dropbox to the Pi and runs `vt-ilsa-export` (branch bob/ilsa-export): every table to CSV in ~/ilsa-export (stays on the Pi), plus a summary of table names / row counts / columns to paste to Bob. Then: (1) *receipts* imported as closed checks marked ILSA, each line keeping its own name, UPC, dept/brand and price as sold; shown in Look Up Checks (reprint, gift receipt); never in today's drawers, X/Z or end-of-day; (2) *ILSA item reference*: all ~33,000 items read-only (UPCs + alt UPCs, name, mfg/brand/product group, last cost/price, first/last sold); the owner plans to trim the active list to under 20,000; a scan or name search not on file at the register, in receiving or in Stock Count finds it there: Restore (with its details), Write off (count only), or just look; a count ends with a "found and restored" list; (3) *trim candidates*: items with no sales since a chosen date / never sold, with on hand and last received, reviewed by brand/department; (4) sales history feeds the Movement screen (same weeks last year, seasonality); (5) ILSA vendors and any target margins. *Beta tie-out*: the owner will check ViewTouch reports against ILSA printouts during the BH beta to prove accuracy (and expects to find other accounting/data issues): a side-by-side report for a day or range (sales by department, tax, tenders, discounts, voids/refunds, gift cards, item counts) with a variance column, for imported ILSA days (checks the importer) and parallel beta days.
* **BH coupons and manufacturer coupons as tenders (owner 2026-11-04; not started)**: BH coupon: a customer brings 10 carton UPCs (cut from cartons; no barcode of its own) and gets \$2.50 off a carton; the cashier decides eligibility (no scan check of the UPCs). Taken as a TENDER, like a manufacturer coupon (no change to taxable sales), as in ILSA (dept 440 BUTTHEADS COUPON / 460 MFG COUPONS history). Build: a "BH Coupon" tender fixed at \$2.50 (several per sale allowed) and an "MFG Coupon" tender with the amount typed; each BH coupon used prints its own redemption slip for auditing ("BH COUPON REDEMPTION \$2.50", date, receipt #, carton sold, cashier, room to staple the 10 UPCs); coupon counts and \$ per day/month in reports and the ILSA tie-out. Manufacturer coupon barcodes (GS1 DataBar, AI 8110): USB handheld scanners read them (DS9808 does), the tablet camera (ML Kit) can't; parse the offer code, cashier confirms the amount.
* **ILSA import: what the BH Danbury data is and the cutover plan (owner 2026-11-04)**: Source: `copy bhdan 10-29-2026.mdb` (owner's Google Drive, Neil's folder; on the Pi in ~/Downloads; CSVs in ~/ilsa-export/, never in the repo). History 2014-03-21..2026-10-29, one register, ~4,600 daily Z's, 415,506 receipts (RegSale; key REG_NUM+RECEIPT_NUM unique), 855,867 receipt lines (PDetail rows that match a receipt), 402,509 tenders (all match). Owner: older (2009-2014) data may come later; no receiving was done in ILSA; only sales and movement data matter; receipts carry no customer data, only the buyer's DOB (AGE_VERIFIED: 18 before 2019-10-01, 21 after). *Changed 2026-11-06 (owner decision): import the buyer's DOB with each legacy receipt too, and print it on legacy receipts when recalled, as ILSA did. The history DB stays on the Pi (never in the repo, which is public; tests use made-up dates).* Line codes (PDetail DETAIL_CLASS,DETAIL_TYPE): 1,1 item sales (+$17.48M); 1,6 negative dept entries (lottery/online redemptions, comp lottery, mfg coupons; -$468k); 15,<PRC_ADJ id> promotions/buydowns (cigarettes; -$74k); 16,<id> small promo reversals; 30,30 unknown (+$303k, 7,351 lines; check against Z); 4,1 dept-0 entries (= MDetail class 4, $129k); 9,15 not on receipts (= InvAdj type 15, ILSA stock moves): skip. MODE_FLAGS: 1/128 pairs = voided lines (net 0); 256 = returns; 4096, 32768 to confirm. RegSale Z_MODE: 0 normal ($17.8M), 1 = 4,497 receipts $306,592 (unknown: training? check with vt-ilsa-tieout), 2 = 11,590 receipts at $0. Tenders: 1 Cash, 10 Personal Check, 15 BH Gift Card, 70 Credit Cards. Tax: STANDARD 6.35%. Trim estimate (year of last PLU sale): 2026 4,378; 2025 2,250; 2024 1,667; 2023 1,464; 2019-2022 ~4,200; 2014-2018 ~4,100; never sold 15,479 (keep items with stock on hand regardless). ILSA's own Z numbers (Z_Totals/Z_Tender/Z_Taxes) let the importer check itself on every day. Of the \$235,651 total cash gap, \$230,003.00 is ONE 2023 day (ILSA's Z cash inflated: likely a mistyped amount); the other ~580 days are small (-\$280..+\$1,083), consistent with the owner's ILSA crash days (one journal didn't write to the other): import receipts as they are and list those days on a "Known ILSA differences" report. Paid outs: MDetail class 4 = PDetail 4,1 lines = RegReconcile Z_PAYOUTS (\$18,496.56 on the gap days; \$128,917 in all): import as paid outs (not sales, not tenders). Receipt modes 1 (\$306,592, no tenders) and 2 (\$0) are not sales (likely cancelled sales / no sales; ILSA's Z counters for these are all 0). *Source files (owner 2026-11-05: use these to reconstruct the history)*: on the Pi in ~/Downloads: `copy bhdan 10-29-2026.mdb` (2014-03-21..2026-10-29), `ILSA.mdb_CopyDB_jan2006_to_fall2008` (2006-12-10..2011-08-02), `Copy of ILSA.mdb` (740 MB: 2008-11-06..2012-12-13, 239,608 receipts; 2009 and 2010 identical to the 2006-2011 file, adds 2011-08..2012-12-13), `ILSA - Copy.mdb` (960 MB: 2008-11-06..2013-07-23, 267,086 receipts; contains everything in `Copy of ILSA.mdb` plus 2012-12-14..2013-07-23). `ILSA-kpbhdanmissing.mdb` (1.1 GB: 2008-11-06..2015-01-24; 2009-2012 identical to the others; fills 2013-07-24..2014-03-20; overlaps copy bhdan 2014-03-21..2015-01-24): CONFIRMED (vt-ilsa-compare 2026-11-05): all four files are BUTTHEAD'S TOBACCO EMPORIUM store 1, DANBURY, CT; every shared day identical (kp vs copy bhdan: 308/308; kp vs ILSA - Copy: 1,709/1,710, the exception 2013-07-23 = the copy was made mid-day; kp vs 2006 file: 992/992). *Import from (one source per day, no gaps)*: 2006-12-10..2008-11-05 `ILSA.mdb_CopyDB_jan2006_to_fall2008`; 2008-11-06..2014-03-20 `ILSA-kpbhdanmissing.mdb`; 2014-03-21..2026-10-29 (and the fresh copy at cutover) `copy bhdan 10-29-2026.mdb`. About 836,000 receipts. `ILSA - Copy.mdb` is not needed (inside kp). *Owner 2026-11-05: retain the completed history; at import time he provides the latest ILSA copy and Bob merges its up-to-date data.* Plan: build the history once into ~/ilsa-history/ilsa-history.db (SQLite on the Pi, never in the repo: receipts, lines, tenders, paid outs, items, departments, ILSA Z's; each receipt tagged with its source file) with its self-check report; at cutover merge the latest copy: for every day the latest copy has, its data replaces the stored day (a copy's last day can be partial, e.g. 2013-07-23), then import the whole history into ViewTouch. Not used: `ilsa.mdb` (2.9 MB, empty), `Copy of ILSA.mdb` (subset of `ILSA - Copy.mdb`), `PosReceipts.mdb` (ILSA's rolling last-7-days receipt text for register reprints; has a PAN column: never import). The importer takes all of them, labels each receipt with its source database, and skips receipts that appear in more than one. *Older database (2026-11-05)*: `ILSA.mdb_CopyDB_jan2006_to_fall2008` (1.2 GB, on the Pi in ~/Downloads; CSVs in ~/ilsa-export/ILSA) actually holds 2006-12-10..2011-08-02: 292,278 receipts (\$7.57M), 644,524 lines, 285,228 tenders, one register, same table layout and codes. No overlap with the 2014 database (gap 2011-08-03..2014-03-20 still missing). Receipt numbers may repeat across databases: key imported receipts by source database + register + receipt #. Same rules hold: checks 1,248/1,248 days, gift cards 92/92, cards (70) 1,190/1,190 to the cent with AMT_TEND; cash AMT_TEND - CHANGE_DUE off by only \$8,042 in total (one mistyped huge tendered amount makes raw AMT_TEND useless for cash). Extra tender types then: 40 OLD CC (to 2010-03-16, then 70), 80 American Express, 12 House Charge Account, 0 (160 rows, Dec 2009): map 40/80 to card, 12 to house charge. *Cutover plan (owner)*: about two weeks of checking until the data is clean; then wipe ViewTouch and import the good data from a fresh ILSA copy. *Changed 2026-11-06 (owner approved): at cutover wipe only the beta's sales and transactions; KEEP the item list (with the names, sizes, makers and groups fixed in ViewTouch); the importer adds only items new in ILSA (matched by UPC, names left alone) and imports the sales history onto the kept items; the 15-minute price sync updates prices by UPC only, never names.* So the importer must be a repeatable script (export -> import -> self-check), run with ViewTouch stopped, with a backup of viewtouch.db first. *Owner 2026-11-07 (transition checklist):* (1) ILSA history is in (2006-12-10..2026-10-29, three copies; ~/ilsa-history/ilsa-history.db). At cutover: export the final ILSA copy and run `vt-ilsa-history <final export> --from 2026-10-29` so it picks up from the last day imported (2026-10-29 is kept as partial and replaced). (2) Wipe every ViewTouch transaction made during the beta (the owner's test checks, payments, drawers, time clock tests), keeping items, settings, staff, pages and the ILSA history file. Needs a 'wipe beta transactions' step with a backup first (not built yet). (3) Parallel testing until then: the owner rings a selection of transactions in ViewTouch alongside the ILSA register, not every sale, so whole days won't match; the goal is to try many kinds of transactions (voids, returns, discounts, gift cards, split tenders, age checks, buydowns) and check ViewTouch's behavior and reports transaction by transaction.
* **Tablet kiosk: Bluetooth pairing and "Close ViewTouch" (found 2026-11-05)**: opening Android Bluetooth settings from the Pi (adb am start android.settings.BLUETOOTH_SETTINGS) fails with error 101 (lock task mode). Pairing the Unitech MS912+ needed Manager > Close ViewTouch, and after that a reboot did NOT bring ViewTouch back (Kiosk.leave clears the HOME preference and disables the Home component; KioskBoot's start from BOOT_COMPLETED is blocked as a background start); fixed by starting QtActivity with adb or tapping the icon (Kiosk.apply re-locks). To do in the next APK: a "Bluetooth scanner" button (Site Setup > Tablets, and on the tablet) that temporarily allows com.android.settings in setLockTaskPackages and opens Bluetooth settings, then returns; and keep HOME so a reboot after Close ViewTouch returns to ViewTouch.
* **ILSA price book sync (built 2026-11-05; automatic in ViewTouch since 2026-11-07)**: *Now automatic (owner 2026-11-07, branch bob/ilsa-sync): the Pi's ViewTouch (the one serving the store, vt-server --serve) runs vt-ilsa-pricebook --exit-status every 15 minutes (first run a minute after start); when a new export came in (or the latest wasn't synced yet: ~/ilsa-sync/last-synced.txt) it runs vt-ilsa-items and applies ~/ilsa-items/viewtouch-menu.json with PosService::syncPriceBook: new ILSA items ADDED (same conversion rules, into their department; id ilsa-<PLU>); PRICES of existing items follow ILSA, and since 2026-11-07 (branch bob/audit) so do COSTS: vt-ilsa-items sends PLU.LAST_COST (the owner's system cost, list + OTP, kept in ILSA; ILSA copies it into each sale line's ITEM_COST) as the item's cost, new items get it, an existing item's cost follows it (logged 'cost old -> new'), an empty ILSA cost never clears a ViewTouch cost; each sale line freezes its cost when rung, so a cost change never alters past sales; new barcodes ILSA has for an item are added; NAMES, sizes, category, tax and everything else set in ViewTouch never change (ILSA name / department / tax differences and items gone from ILSA are only logged, once each); items are matched by ANY barcode, then by the ilsa-<PLU> id, never by name; an item two ILSA items point at keeps its price when they disagree (logged); nothing merged or deleted. No login needed (Changes report: "ILSA sync"); one menu revision per run; open checks keep their prices. Logs in ~/ilsa-sync: sync-log.csv, runs.log, price-changes-<time>.csv. Site Setup > ILSA price book: On/Off (Store Settings' ilsaSyncOn, default on), last run, Sync now, Show log. Menu Builder > Import a menu is no longer needed for the price book.* The ILSA Windows 7 PC (Samsung Slate Pro, C:\ilsa\data\ilsacorp.mdb) runs "ViewTouch ILSA Sync" every 15 minutes (tools/ilsa-windows; Jet text export, seconds) and sends PLU, PRC_DATA, PRC_NAME, Inv_Info, Dept, Dept_Grp, Pos_Flag to Dropbox\POS project\ilsa-pricebook only when they change; the Pi pulls with scripts/bin/vt-ilsa-pricebook. Neil's "ILSA Price book sync" task (every 15 min, failing since 11-03) to be deleted by the owner. First book: 33,552 items; PLU.LAST_PRICE equals the PRC_DATA price on every item (one price per item). The ACTIVE flag is useless (owner: ignore it; ILSACorp shows 7,327 active / 26,225 inactive): Owner 2026-11-05: import ALL ILSA items (lots of old stock); do NOT import quantities on hand (movement comes from the history); wants what sold at what price and GP on cost at the time CONFIRMED (vt-ilsa-costcheck 2026-11-05): each ILSA sale line (PDetail 1/1) has unit price DETAIL_ITEM_PRC, quantity DETAIL_FOR_QTY (DETAIL_AT_QTY unused), unit cost at the time ITEM_COST (AVG_COST empty); line cost = ITEM_COST x DETAIL_FOR_QTY. Merchandise GP (lottery out, no-cost lines out): 2014 26.9%, 2015-2019 29-30%, 2020-2022 30-31%, 2023-2026 32.0-32.2%, matching the owner's expectation (~25% rising to 30-32%). Lines without a cost are almost all dept 260 GM OTHER GM (\$2k-\$75k a year): import as "no cost", left out of GP and shown separately. Lottery: instant tickets (270, 275) carry cost (5% GP); online lottery depts have none; lottery sales ended in 2023; kept as their own departments, out of merchandise GP.
* **33,000-item catalog speed (test run 42112495087, CI machine, 2026-11-05)**: import via importMenuFile 1.1 s; item list built 0.19 s (0.34 s per later call: copied each time); save all 0.5 s; load 0.15 s: fine. NOT fine: (1) a barcode scan takes 0.36 s (LayoutController::scanned walks every item's QVariantMap and splits its barcodes string; ~1 s on the Pi); (2) the item list is 26.7 MB as JSON and is synced whole to every tablet (menuItems, Group::Admin) on each menu change, so every 15-minute price sync would push ~27 MB to each tablet. Before importing the ILSA price book: (a) a code -> item index on the server (hash, rebuilt when the menu changes) used by scans everywhere (tablets ask the server); (b) tablets get a slim item list (no 33,000 retail items; search and labels ask the server). Also add manufacturer / product group / sub group to MenuItem so ViewTouch keeps the ILSA tree (the Labels tree uses the ILSA price book today).
* **Departments going forward (owner 2026-11-05)**: of Newspapers, Magazines, Beverages, Candy, Food, Vapor, Event Tickets, only MAGAZINES and EVENT TICKETS continue. VAPOR is eliminated now (owner 2026-11-05): vt-ilsa-items leaves departments 55/56 out (history only), like lottery. The others (and lottery) are history only; the owner deletes them "at the appropriate time" (import them as they are for now; retire later, history keeps them). vt-ilsa-items found 31,906 items in 22 categories (lottery's 1,646 left out), 558 same-name groups (1,161 items), 8,745 items on the owner's 708694 codes (4,175 never sold since 2014; 72 with a same-name maker-UPC twin).
* **Owner 2026-11-05: NO automatic merges; in-house UPCs; history follows UPC changes**: every ILSA item imports as its own ViewTouch item; the owner decides which UPCs to merge (vt-ilsa-items writes possible-duplicates.csv for review). 708694xxxxxx is the owner's own GS1 UPC range: he wants to free as many as possible (inhouse-upcs.csv: in-house codes, last sale, same-name items with a maker's UPC = candidates). History must follow later merges/re-codes "retroactively": keep every sale line's original UPC untouched and add a UPC map (code, from date, to date -> ViewTouch item) maintained by the merge / re-code actions; reports and movement read history through the map. Date ranges matter because a freed in-house code reused for a new product must not pull the old product's sales onto the new one. *Retiring items and reusing in-house codes (owner 2026-11-05)*: an item with sales history is never hard-deleted: Retire hides it from selling, search, labels and reorder; its history stays; scanning one of its codes offers Restore (items with no history can be deleted outright). Retiring releases its 708694 codes: the UPC map entry gets an end date and the code goes to a free-code pool (only after a cool-off since its last sale, owner's choice, e.g. 12 months, and never while ViewTouch thinks stock is on the shelf); a new item can take the next free in-house code (check digit worked out; labels from the Labels screen); sales before the end date stay with the old item, after it with the new one. *Full-store physical inventory (owner 2026-11-05; process TO BE AGREED with the owner, based on what's selling)*: most in-house 708694 codes date from years ago when many makers didn't barcode; the owner will review the legacy UPCs, and a scan of the entire store will clear them up. Likely shape (not decided): every item on the shelf scanned and counted; afterwards ViewTouch lists in-house codes NOT found and not sold since a chosen date (retire, code freed), in-house items found that now have a maker's barcode (relabel or merge, history follows through the UPC map), and items found but not on file (restore from the ILSA reference or add). *Tree restructuring the same way (owner 2026-11-05)*: history lines also keep the department / manufacturer / product group / sub group as sold; reports group history by the item's CURRENT tree (code -> item through the UPC map -> its department and brand today), so moving items between departments, splitting or regrouping departments, or renaming a manufacturer re-groups all past sales automatically; plus name maps (e.g. SWED MATCH -> SWEDISH MATCH, old department -> new) for codes no longer on any item; an "as recorded then" view stays available for the ILSA tie-out.
* **ILSA cleanup: duplicate items and box sizes (owner 2026-11-04; part of the ILSA import)**: (1) *Duplicates*: alt UPC never worked in ILSA, so many products exist as several items with different UPCs. During the import, find likely duplicates (same brand/size/price, names alike after normalizing abbreviations, items that never sold at the same time); the owner reviews groups and picks the name; the kept item gets every UPC (one main, the rest alt UPCs), the combined sales history and on hand; old receipts unchanged; merges logged and undoable until the import is final. (2) *Box sizes and box inventory* (owner sells many boxes and must know full-box inventory, so NOT a single stick total): each cigar keeps separate on-hand counts: sealed boxes per size (20, 24... each size has its own unique UPC, so a scan always knows) and loose singles. Receiving a box: that size's sealed +1; box sale: sealed -1; single sale: loose -1. *Open a box*: cashiers tap it whenever they open one (owner wants it done then): pick the size, sealed -1, loose +20/24. Loose can be more than one box (e.g. a 10-count box with 12 on the shelf so a 3-5 cigar sale never leaves 2): no limit on loose. Fallback when a single sale would take loose below 0: "No loose singles on hand. Did you open a box? [Box of 20] [Box of 24] [Not now]" (doesn't hold up the sale). Recording doesn't have to happen at the shelf: an *EMPTIED A BOX* button on the Cashier Duty screen (retail mode) opens the box-opening screen; scan each empty box's barcode (its UPC gives cigar and size), several in one go ("empty box back to the counter, scan it, toss it"); a humidor attendant at HCA switches a tablet to retail mode and does it there (tablet camera Scan works). Optional per-item *shelf minimum* for loose singles: a reminder to open a box when below it. Reports: box inventory (sealed by brand/size, value), movement split into boxes sold / singles sold / boxes opened, reorder in boxes; Stock Count counts sealed per size and loose separately (differences show as a missing box vs missing singles). Total sticks only for valuation.
* **ID scan for age-restricted sales (first version built 2026-11-04; refine with the owner when BH Danbury runs beta beside ILSA later in November)**: Scan the license barcode (PDF417/AAMVA) on each tobacco, cigarette, rolling-paper, e-cig and alcohol sale; block under the Store Settings > Age-restricted sales minimums (ageTobacco / ageAlcohol, already there) or an expired ID; log only "ID checked, 21+, who, when" on the check. The owner wants an ID from every tobacco buyer, so a guest-screen PIN can't replace the ID. The owner has more requirements for this, including IDs whose barcode won't read or that have none: ask him before starting. Needs a 2D scanner.
* **Upstream sync ledger (official ViewTouch: github.com/ViewTouch/viewtouch)**: the fork's base is d9ad3d0a (2026-10-15). Policy (owner left it to Bob, 2026-11-04): stay a downstream fork; bring upstream fixes in small groups, each tested on GitHub (Debian 13, Qt 6.8) before the Pi; record each upstream id in the Log; no Qt 6.12 work while the Pi runs Qt 6.8; offer good general fixes of ours back upstream. * **4-Node Multi-Store Hub & Web Portal**: `store_config.json` and `corp_sync.py` across `CORP_HUB`, `HCAH_DANBURY`, `BHT_DANBURY`, and `BHT_GAYLORDSVILLE`, plus InMotion PHP/MySQL online ordering with Pi background polling and ESC/POS kitchen auto-printing (`192.168.1.77:9100`).
* **SMS texting and customer follow-ups (owner 2026-11-07, idea)**: Today: Store Settings > Waitlist and texts > Texting service URL (POSTs JSON {to, message}); used by the waitlist Notify (editable 'table-ready text', {name}/{store}) and texted receipts (fixed wording). Planned: (1) a relay on the Pi for Twilio (account keys only on the Pi, never in the repo); (2) every text editable in Store Settings as a template with fields ({name}, {store}, {check}, {total}, {event}, {date}, {balance}, {link}); (3) customer sign-up by cell phone at the register, with a follow-up text asking for their email for the weekly events list (reply with the email, or a link to a short form); (4) per-customer text choices recorded with date and how given: receipts/notices, events and offers, or none; (5) event reminders: pick an event, a message and the customers who chose event texts (by store, subscription, last visit), send now or schedule; (6) opt-outs: a STOP reply is caught by Twilio and sent back to the relay, which marks the customer 'no texts' at once (shown on their record, with the date), and every later send skips them; START turns it back on; (7) a texts report: sent, delivered, failed, opt-outs, by event. Ties into the cross-store customer list (phone is the key) and the HCA subscription program. *Phone changes (owner 2026-11-07)*: the customer record keeps its own id, so history, points, subscription and gift cards stay with the person; editing the phone keeps the old number on the record as 'previous' (not used for lookups or texts once someone else has it; a scan of a receipt/card still finds the person); text choices belong to the number: the new number starts with event texts off until the customer says yes again, a STOP on the old number stays with the old number; if the new number already belongs to another record, offer to merge the two; the change is logged (who, when) and syncs to every store.
* **One customer across all stores (owner 2026-11-07, planned with the corporate Pi)**: a customer is the same person at BH Danbury, HCA and any future site. One shared customer list kept by the corporate Pi, with a copy on every site Pi so lookups and new customers work when the VPN is down (synced when it's back). Match key: the cell phone number (owner's choice); staff confirm the name; two records with the same phone are merged (history follows). At any register (bar or store), the customer's Past transactions / Past items show purchases from every location, newest first, each marked with its store (e.g. BH Danbury, HCA); *Owner 2026-11-07 16:57: a synchronized transaction file across all stores with a check prefix*: every check number carries its store's prefix (e.g. BH-104233, HCA-5012) so numbers never collide; each store writes only its own checks, and closed checks are copied (read-only) through the corporate Pi to every other store, so any register can look up and reprint another store's check offline; a sale is never changed at a store that didn't ring it (returns of another store's sale are rung as a new check that points to the original). ILSA history has no customer on its receipts, so purchases from before ViewTouch can't be tied to a person: for those, look up by item, UPC, date or total on Look Up Checks > ILSA history. Fits the HCA subscription program (pricing at bar and retail).
* **One customer across all stores (owner 2026-11-07, planned with the corporate Pi)**: a customer is the same person at BH Danbury, HCA and any future site. One shared customer list kept by the corporate Pi, with a copy on every site Pi so lookups and new customers work when the VPN is down (synced when it's back). At any register (bar or store), the customer's Past transactions / Past items show purchases from every location, newest first, each marked with its store (e.g. BH Danbury, HCA); *Owner 2026-11-07 16:57: a synchronized transaction file across all stores with a check prefix*: every check number carries its store's prefix (HCA Hat City Ale House, BHDAN Butthead's Danbury, BHNM Butthead's New Milford = Gaylordsville; e.g. BHDAN-104233, HCA-5012) so numbers never collide; the imported ILSA history already shows its receipts this way (BHDAN-836100: legacy_sources.store, set by vt-ilsa-history --prefix); each store writes only its own checks, and closed checks are copied (read-only) through the corporate Pi to every other store, so any register can look up and reprint another store's check offline; a sale is never changed at a store that didn't ring it (returns of another store's sale are rung as a new check that points to the original). ILSA history has no customer on its receipts, so purchases from before ViewTouch can't be tied to a person: for those, look up by item, UPC, date or total on Look Up Checks > ILSA history. Fits the HCA subscription program (pricing at bar and retail).

### 2026-11-07 17:26 ET — Bob (Claude) — Receipt barcode: scan a receipt to open its check (returns)
- **What:** Store Settings > Receipts: 'Print a barcode on receipts' (receiptBarcode, default off) and 'Check prefix' (checkPrefix, default empty; letters/digits, capitals, up to 8, e.g. HCA, BHDAN, BHNM). On: receipts, reprints and gift receipts end with a Code 128 barcode of 'R' + prefix + '-' + check number (R-5012, RHCA-5012): ESC/POS GS k 73 (code set B) with its text under it (GS H 2); plain-text/on-screen receipts show the reference centered; the receipt picture (Exceptions PDF) draws the bars. New Document::Kind::Barcode and print::code128Modules; core/receipt_ref.hh parses/builds the reference. A scan of it on the order/Retail screens, Pay, Find a check, Reprint Receipt or Look Up Checks opens Find a check with that check selected (PosService::openReceiptCode, any day), never an item; Refund... there as before (manager). Typing the reference in Find a check's search does the same. Another store's prefix: 'That receipt is from <prefix>; look it up there.'; no such check: 'No check #N here.'; a receipt without prefix at a store with one: asks to check the store name and type #N. Spanish strings added. Tests: tests/test_receipt_barcode.cpp (made-up checks).
- **Why:** Owner 2026-11-07: a barcode at the bottom of receipts to retrieve the old receipt and do returns from it (rarely needed, but wanted).

### 2026-11-07 16:18 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-ilsa-history --recheck: the self-check redone from the history file, with explanations
- **What:** New vt-ilsa-history --recheck [--dry-run] [--from/--to]: rebuilds every day's totals and differences from ilsa-history.db (no export, nothing imported; a backup first unless --dry-run). The receipt check now allows the receipt discount (RT_AMT_DISCOUNTS, either sign) and a cent of rounding, and prints how every receipt was explained plus the most common unexplained differences. Tenders are compared only on Z's where ILSA saved tenders (the others are counted by year). Z_Totals columns may match a total with either sign and receipt discounts. For the Z's that still differ on tax or cash it tests what the difference equals (tax on cancelled sales, on returns, on negative receipts, on receipts dated another day; change on card/gift/check tenders, cash given back, paid outs...). A Dates section lists each copy's days that stand apart from its other days with their Z's, run times and receipt dates, and the Z's whose receipts span more than a day. 2023-02-02 Z 5828's cash is labelled as the known mistyped amount. The import uses the same check. Made-up test data gained a receipt with a receipt discount.
- **Why:** Owner's first real import 2026-11-07: the receipt check failed on almost every Z (it ignored receipt discounts), cash differed on Z's ILSA kept no tenders for, and the history started in 2004.
- **Files:** `modern/tests/legacy_fixture.inc`, `modern/tests/test_legacy.cpp`, `scripts/bin/vt-ilsa-history`
- **Next person must know:** Run it on the Pi with --dry-run first and paste the output; nothing about the 2004 days is changed until its Dates section is read.

### 2026-11-07 14:29 ET — Bob (Claude) — Billboards: power the TV off outside its hours (adb sleep / wake)
- **What:** New billboard setting 'Power the TV off outside its hours' (Billboard defaults: on, for Fire TVs; a TV of another known make is off unless set). The store's server checks every 30 s (and at once after a change): outside its hours or Off now it runs vt-add-billboard --sleep (adb KEYCODE_SLEEP); at their start or On now --wake (KEYCODE_WAKEUP, 5 s, am start of the billboard). Not connected 60 s after waking: woken once more, then its row says "Didn't come back: check the TV's Wake on Wi-Fi setting" (On now tries again). Setting off or wake failed: the screen just goes black as before. Rows say asleep / waking / dark / on; Off now and On now buttons on its form. Each power step is logged (journal: 'Billboard power: ...') and shown in its job box. A failed sleep is tried again 10 minutes later.
- **Why:** Owner tested on fire5 (Insignia Fire TV): adb's sleep key puts the TV in standby and the wake key brings it back on the Fire home screen, so the app must be started again.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_billboards.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_json.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.hh`, `modern/core/settings.hh`, `modern/i18n/es.json`, `modern/main.cpp`, `modern/tests/test_billboard.cpp`, `modern/ui/qml/WidgetAdminPanel.qml`, `scripts/bin/vt-add-billboard`
- **Next person must know:** Only the store's server powers TVs (main.cpp: shared->billboardPowerOn). A TV turned on by hand outside its hours is put back to sleep within a minute or so (5 minutes after it was slept, once it connects); use On now instead.

Site Setup has a new ILSA price book box: On/Off (default on, kept in the store settings as ilsaSyncOn), last run and result, Sync now, Show log. vt-ilsa-pricebook --exit-status exits 3 when the export is the same as last time (normal output unchanged without the flag). Tests: tests/test_ilsa_sync.cpp, including a 33,000-item timing test.

### 2026-11-06 11:26 ET — Bob (Claude) — Scanner: a UPC read with a 0 in front still finds the item
- **What:** Hand port of the scanner part of upstream ed80209. New PosService::sameBarcode(): a 13-digit code starting with 0 matches the 12-digit UPC on file (and the other way round), and the existing 11-digit (no check digit) match moved into it. Used by itemForCode (every scan on the register and tablets), findItem and the Item / UPC Builder search; exact matches still win first. Test added in test_customers.cpp. The upstream commit's other parts (new-item-from-unknown-code card, spreadsheet Cost column) were not taken.
- **Why:** Some scanners read a UPC-A as an EAN-13 and send a 0 in front, so the item came up as not on file.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_loyalty.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_menu.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.hh`, `modern/tests/test_customers.cpp`

### 2026-11-06 11:24 ET — Bob (Claude) — Server refuses odd input from screens
- **What:** Ported upstream df89b9f (app code only; its new test file was left out). A kitchen screen can only be set to one of the store's stations (or none). Opening a table refuses an empty name, a name over 40 characters, or one with spaces at the ends. Posted messages can be at most a month ahead and twenty at a time. The command list moved into PosService::commandTable() with commandNames() for tests; invoke() works the same.
- **Why:** A screen could send any text as its station or table name (an empty or 5,000-character table showed up on the floor, receipts and reports), and posted messages had no limit, piling up in the settings every screen receives.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_commands.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_kitchen.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.cpp`, `modern/app/pos_service.hh`

### 2026-11-06 11:23 ET — Bob (Claude) — Labor report total equals the sum of its rows
- **What:** Ported upstream f510043. The Labor report rounds each person's pay to the cent first and the total is those rows added up, plus a test with three half-hour shifts at $10.01.
- **Why:** The total was rounded once at the end, so it could be a cent or two different from adding up the rows.
- **Files:** `modern/core/report.cpp`, `modern/tests/test_reports.cpp`

### 2026-11-06 11:22 ET — Bob (Claude) — Startup reads the store's tables in one go
- **What:** Ported upstream 9514708. When ViewTouch starts, it reads all of the store's tables (about thirty) inside one read transaction instead of one by one.
- **Why:** The background writer could save between two of those reads, mixing data from just before with data from just after; one read is also faster.
- **Files:** `modern/storage/pos_store.cpp`

### 2026-11-06 11:22 ET — Bob (Claude) — Forget Customer no longer freezes the screen
- **What:** Ported upstream 8d98a74. Forget Customer removes the record and today's checks at once; the earlier days' checks are searched in the background and cleaned up afterwards, then it says how many checks it cleared. Test updated to wait for that message.
- **Why:** Searching every check the store ever kept on the screen's thread held the screen for seconds on a Pi with years of sales.
- **Files:** `modern/app/pos_customers.cpp`, `modern/tests/test_safety.cpp`

### 2026-11-06 11:21 ET — Bob (Claude) — Backup restore: never leaves the store without a database
- **What:** Ported upstream 7833c83 (backup.cpp only). The data set aside before a restore always gets a name nobody else has, and if the database, its -wal or its -shm can't be moved aside, the ones already moved are put back.
- **Why:** Two restores in the same second clashed, and a half-finished move could leave the store with no database, so the next start would begin an empty one.
- **Files:** `modern/storage/backup.cpp`

### 2026-11-04 14:05 ET — Bob (Claude) — ID checks for tobacco and alcohol (license scan)
- **What:** Items in the tobacco categories ticked in Site Setup > ID checks (idCheckTobacco, tobaccoCategories, ageTobacco) and, when on, items taxed as alcohol (idCheckAlcohol, ageAlcohol) wait for the buyer's ID: AgeCheckOverlay (POS and tablets) says scan the back of the ID; core/id_card.hh reads the AAMVA PDF417 (DBB birth, DBA expiry, DAQ number; US MMDDCCYY or Canadian CCYYMMDD); under age or expired: refused (logged on the check), else the check keeps ageVerified/ageHow/ageBy/ageAt/ageId4 (last 4 only) and the item goes in; later restricted items on that check go straight in. 'Won't scan: type date' = verifyAgeHand (MMDDYYYY), logged as the exception ('ageHand' event), optionally manager PIN (idHandNeedsManager). Scans: SerialScanner keeps a license's line breaks (buffer starting '@'); keyboard-mode scanners (Bluetooth on a tablet) the same via Main.qml (Shift no longer ends a burst; idScanEnd timer). A license's small 1D barcode (Code 128/39) is ignored while an ID check waits and within 3 s of a license scan, and a license scan closes an 'Item not on file' the small one opened, so the scanner can keep reading all barcode kinds (cigar labels). ### 2026-11-04 09:39 ET — Bob (Claude) — vt-directv rebuilt: no database writes, no frozen screen, TV scan
- **What:** scripts/bin/vt-directv rewritten. Same command line for the DIRECTV pages (box|all tune <ch> / key <key>), but it no longer rebuilds pages into viewtouch.db and seed/pages after every tune (--init-pages now just says so). TV list moved to ~/.config/viewtouch/tvs.json (built-in five bar receivers if missing). New: 'vt-directv scan [--save] [subnet]' finds DIRECTV receivers and smart TVs by SSDP plus a sweep of this computer's subnets and the known TVs' subnets (DIRECTV 8080, Roku 8060, Samsung 8001, LG webOS 3000, Android/Google 8008); 'list'. Roku TVs can be worked now (ECP keypress); Samsung/LG/Sony/Android TV are found and listed but need a one-time pairing (not built yet). 'all' sends to every receiver at once. LayoutController runs it in the background with a 10 s limit instead of waiting on the UI thread.

Audit: writing viewtouch.db while ViewTouch runs breaks the one-writer rule, overwrote layout edits, rewrote repo files on the Pi (blocking git pull), and the 4 s wait froze the POS when a box was off.
- **Files:** `scripts/bin/vt-directv, modern/ui/cpp/layoutcontroller.cpp`
- **Next person must know:** Pi: sudo ln -sf ~/viewtouch-main/scripts/bin/vt-directv /usr/local/bin/vt-directv so git pull keeps it current. ### 2026-11-04 09:30 ET — Bob (Claude) — Audit fixes: real build numbers, graceful restart, barcodes save
- **What:** Removed the hardcoded build 19 / 0.9.19 lines from modern/cmake/BuildInfo.cmake so each build gets its real git count again. vt-server and vt-fast now stop vtmodern with SIGTERM, wait up to 10 s, and only then force it. Menu item barcodes are no longer refused for an 11-digit code or a GS1 check digit that doesn't add up (shape and duplicate checks stay).
- **Why:** Audit of earlier changes: the fixed build number made every Pi and tablet look the same version to the update check and to the server's behind/ahead compare; killall -9 could cut a database write in half; the check-digit block meant store and vendor codes that don't follow GS1 could never be saved, and the 11-digit message promised a fill-in that never happened.

### 2026-11-02 16:38 ET — Bob (Claude) — Tablets: wireless debugging back on after a restart
- **What:** Kiosk.ensureWirelessAdb (runs at boot): when the app holds WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS, it sets adb_enabled, adb_wifi_enabled and adb_allowed_connection_time itself (Settings.Global). The existing DevicePolicyManager.setGlobalSetting calls for those keys always failed silently: Android doesn't let a device owner change them. Manifest declares the permission. One-time per tablet over USB: adb shell pm grant org.viewtouch.pos android.permission.WRITE_SECURE_SETTINGS (kept across app updates).
- **Why:** Owner: Bar 3 (192.168.1.210) stopped taking ADB; tcpip 5555 doesn't survive a tablet restart, so tablets drop off after every reboot.
- **Files:** `modern/android/AndroidManifest.xml`, `modern/android/src/org/viewtouch/pos/Kiosk.kt`
- **Next person must know:** Needs a new APK. With the grant + a paired Pi, wireless debugging returns after each restart and vt-adb-ensure switches it to 5555. Also reserve .220/.215/.210 for the tablets in the router (DHCP reservations) so their addresses never change.

### 2026-11-02 10:48 ET — Bob (Claude) — UI tests: load Main.qml the way the app does
- **What:** test_pos_flow.cpp's Screen now loads Main.qml through loadViewTouchQml(): adds qrc:/ and qrc:/qt/qml import paths and loads the file from :/qt/qml/ViewTouch/ui/qml/ or :/ViewTouch/ui/qml/ (as main.cpp's vtmLoadModuleQml), falling back to loadFromModule.
- **Why:** After b247aa1 the Pi still failed with 'Module ViewTouch contains no type named Main': without the QTP0001 policy the module lives under qrc:/ViewTouch, which loadFromModule doesn't search. The app already works around this; the tests didn't.
- **Files:** `modern/tests/test_pos_flow.cpp`
- **Next person must know:** Other UI tests (test_pages_ui.cpp, test_editor_ui.cpp) still call loadFromModule and will fail the same way on the Pi; only the screenshot path is fixed.
